Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the neuro structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 647. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 530 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_647(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 647. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^530(S, F) is trivial. QED.
